+\lim_{\xi\to x_{1}}\left(x_{1}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{1}^{2}}+2x_{1}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\right)U_{n+1}(\xi,x_{1},J_{n}),

(C.2.18)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{1,1,i}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}\frac{x_{1}x_{i}}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\big\{U_{n+1}(x_{1},x_{1},x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})-U_{n+1}(x_{1},x_{1},J_{n})\big\}\\
+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial x_{1}^{2}\partial x_{i}}\left\{\frac{x_{1}x_{i}U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})-x_{1}^{2}U_{n-1}(J_{n})}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\right\},

(C.2.19)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{1,i,i}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}\left\{\frac{x_{1}x_{i}U_{n+1}(x_{1},x_{1},J_{n})-x_{i}^{2}U_{n+1}(x_{1},x_{i},J_{n})}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\right\},

(C.2.20)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,1,i}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}\\
=\left(x_{i}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}+1\right)\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{1}\partial x_{i}}\left\{\frac{x_{i}U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})-x_{1}U_{n-1}(J_{n})}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\right\},

(C.2.21)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,i,i}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{1}{2x_{1}}\left(x_{i}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}+1\right)\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}^{2}}\left\{\frac{x_{i}^{2}U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})-x_{1}x_{i}U_{n-1}(J_{n})}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\right\}\\
+\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}\frac{x_{i}^{2}}{x_{1}-x_{i}}\big\{U_{n+1}(x_{1},x_{i},J_{n})-U_{n+1}(x_{i},x_{i},J_{n})\big\},

(C.2.22)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{1,i,j}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}x_{i}x_{j}\left\{\frac{U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{j})(x_{i}-x_{j})}-\frac{U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{i}-x_{j})}\right.\\
+\left.\frac{U_{n-1}(x_{1},x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i},x_{j}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{1}-x_{j})}\right\},

(C.2.23)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,1,j}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{1}-x_{j})}\\
\hskip 90.00014pt\times\big\{U_{n-1}(x_{1},x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i},x_{j}\})-U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})\\
+U_{n-1}(J_{n})-U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})\big\},

(C.2.24)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,i,j}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}x_{i}x_{j}\left\{\frac{U_{n-1}(J_{n})}{(x_{1}-x_{j})(x_{i}-x_{j})}-\frac{U_{n-1}(x_{i},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{i}-x_{j})}\right.\\
+\left.\frac{U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{1}-x_{j})}\right\},

(C.2.25)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,j,i}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{i}-x_{j})}\\
\hskip 60.00009pt\times\big\{U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})-U_{n-1}(x_{i},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j}\})\\
+U_{n-1}(J_{n})-U_{n-1}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i}\})\big\},

(C.2.26)

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{\mathcal{A}_{i,j,h}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}\\
\hskip-40.00006pt=\frac{1}{x_{1}}\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}\partial x_{h}}\left\{\frac{x_{i}x_{j}x_{h}U_{n-3}(x_{1},J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i},x_{j},x_{h}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{1}-x_{j})(x_{1}-x_{h})}-\frac{x_{1}x_{j}x_{h}U_{n-3}(J_{n}\setminus\{x_{j},x_{h}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{i})(x_{i}-x_{j})(x_{i}-x_{h})}\right.\\
+\left.\frac{x_{1}x_{i}x_{h}U_{n-3}(J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i},x_{h}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{j})(x_{i}-x_{j})(x_{j}-x_{h})}-\frac{x_{1}x_{i}x_{j}U_{n-3}(J_{n}\setminus\{x_{i},x_{j}\})}{(x_{1}-x_{h})(x_{i}-x_{h})(x_{j}-x_{h})}\right\}.

(C.2.27)

Equation (
C.2.13
) is simply equation (
C.1.4
), while equation (
C.2.14
) can be obtained by applying the operator
-\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}x_{1}
to both sides of equation (
C.1.3
). Likewise, equation (
C.2.17
) can be obtained by first interchanging
x_{1}\leftrightarrow x_{i}
in both sides of equation (
C.1.3
) before applying
-\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{i}}x_{i}
to both sides of said equation. Equation (
C.2.16
) is proven in a similar way to equation (
C.1.6
), with steps relating to the factor
[k_{i}+1]_{\mathrm{mod}\,2}
being skipped. All other computations (
C.2.15
)–(
C.2.27
), bar a caveat on equation (
C.2.18
), can be proven using the methods demonstrated in Appendix
C.1
above and the reasonings just given. We now conclude this appendix with a partial proof of equation (
C.2.18
):

The last term on the right-hand side of equation (
C.2.1
) stands out in that it cannot be treated in the same manner as the other expressions considered in this appendix. Multiplying this term by
x_{1}^{-k_{1}-1}
, summing over
k_{1}\geqslant 0
, and simplifying appropriately shows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}\geqslant 0}2\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}=k_{1}-1}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},p_{3},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k_{1}\geqslant 0}\sum_{p_{1}=0}^{k_{1}-1}\sum_{p_{2}=0}^{k_{1}-p_{1}-1}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},k_{1}-p_{1}-p_{2}-1,I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{p_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k_{1}=p_{1}+1}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{2}=0}^{k_{1}-p_{1}-1}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},k_{1}-p_{1}-p_{2}-1,I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{p_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{2}=0}^{k_{1}}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},k_{1}-p_{2},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+p_{1}+2}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{p_{1},p_{2}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k_{1}=p_{2}}^{\infty}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},k_{1}-p_{2},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+p_{1}+2}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{p_{1},p_{2},k_{1}=0}^{\infty}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},k_{1},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+p_{1}+p_{2}+2}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{q,k_{1}=0}^{\infty}\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=q}p_{1}\frac{m_{q,k_{1},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}x_{1}^{q+1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q,k_{1}=0}^{\infty}q(q+1)\frac{m_{q,k_{1},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}x_{1}^{q+1}}.

Further multiplying this result by
x_{2}^{-k_{2}-1}\cdots x_{n}^{-k_{n}-1}
and summing over
k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0
then shows that

\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}2\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}=k_{1}-1}p_{1}\frac{m_{p_{1}+p_{2},p_{3},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}=\sum_{q,k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}q(q+1)\frac{m_{q,k_{1},I_{n}}}{x_{1}^{k_{1}+1}x_{1}^{q+1}x_{2}^{k_{2}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}\\
=\lim_{\xi\to x_{1}}\left(x_{1}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{1}^{2}}+2x_{1}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\right)\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}\geqslant 0}\frac{m_{q,k_{1},I_{n}}}{\xi^{k_{1}+1}x_{1}^{q+1}x_{2}^{k_{2}+1}\cdots x_{n}^{k_{n}+1}}.

The final expression here is manifestly the third line of equation (
C.2.18
).

Appendix D
Evaluation of Some Cumulants at Leading Order

D.1
Evaluation of
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
,
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}
, and
c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}

As discussed in §
4.2.3
, setting
x(z_{i})=1/(z_{i}^{3}+z_{i})
(
4.2.50
) in the residue formula (
4.2.57
)

c_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),l}=(-1)^{n}\underset{z_{1},\ldots,z_{n}=0}{\mathrm{Res}}W_{n}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),l}(x(z_{1}),\ldots,x(z_{n}))\prod_{i=1}^{n}x(z_{i})^{k_{i}}x^{\prime}(z_{i})\,\mathrm{d}z_{i}

(D.1.1)

and substituting in
W_{1}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}(x(z_{1}))=z_{1}
along with the specifications (
4.2.53
) and (
4.2.52
) of, respectively,
W_{1}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}(x(z_{1}))
and
W_{2}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}(x(z_{1}),x(z_{2}))
enables the computation of the cumulant genus expansion coefficients
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
,
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}
, and
c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
defined implicitly through equation (
4.2.54
).

Letting
\tilde{\mathcal{J}}_{1}
be drawn from the global scaled
(N,N)
antisymmetrised Laguerre ensemble defined in Propositions
3.17
and
3.19
, we have from equations (
1.1.27
) and (
1.1.28
) that

\displaystyle c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\frac{1}{N}\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{J}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\right>,

(D.1.2)

\displaystyle c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\left(\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{J}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\right>-Nc_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}\right);

(D.1.3)

the aforementioned propositions also give combinatorial interpretations of these cumulant expansion coefficients as signed enumerations of particular ribbon graphs and topological hypermaps (see also Figure
4.3
). Due to the antisymmetric nature of
\tilde{\mathcal{J}}_{1}
, the cumulant expansion coefficients
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
and
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}
vanish for odd values of
k_{1}
, while for
k_{1}
even, the former relates to the
m=2
Fuss–Catalan numbers (
1.3.17
) through the relation (
4.2.47
)

c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}=\tilde{M}_{k_{1},0}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1})}=\mathrm{i}^{k_{1}}m_{k_{1}/2}^{(FC_{2})}=\frac{\mathrm{i}^{k_{1}}}{k_{1}+1}\binom{3k_{1}/2}{k_{1}/2}.

(D.1.4)

We now present the values of
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
and
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}
computed through the residue formula (
D.1.1
) with
0\leqslant k_{1}\leqslant 18
even — our calculations are consistent with equation (
D.1.4
) above.

\iff W_{1}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),1}(x(z_{1}))=\frac{x(z_{1})}{3x^{\prime}(z_{1})}\frac{\partial}{\partial z_{1}}W_{1}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}(x(z_{1}))+\frac{1}{3x(z_{1})}=\frac{x(z_{1})}{3x^{\prime}(z_{1})}+\frac{1}{3x(z_{1})},

.

c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}
relates to the analogous quantity
c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(c\mathcal{W}_{2}),0}
of the
(N,N,N)
complex Wishart product ensemble (Definition
1.12
) according to

c_{2k_{1},2k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{J}_{1}),0}=4\,(-1)^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\,c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(c\mathcal{W}_{2}),0},\qquad k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{N},

(D.1.6)

which is in agreement with Proposition
1.8
.

D.2
Evaluation of
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}
,
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),2}
, and
c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}

In parallel to Appendix
D.1
, let us recall from §
4.3.3
that setting
x(z_{i})=(z_{i}^{2}+1)^{2}/z_{i}
(
4.3.34
) in the residue formula (
4.3.38
)

c_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),l}=(-1)^{n}\underset{z_{1},\ldots,z_{n}=0}{\mathrm{Res}}W_{n}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),l}(x(z_{1}),\ldots,x(z_{n}))\prod_{i=1}^{n}x(z_{i})^{k_{i}}x^{\prime}(z_{i})\,\mathrm{d}z_{i}

(D.2.1)

and then substituting in
W_{1}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}(x(z_{1}))=y(z_{1})
(
4.3.33
) along with the expressions for
W_{1}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),2}(x(z_{1}))
and
W_{2}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}(x(z_{1}),x(z_{2}))
given respectively in equations (
4.3.36
) and (
4.3.35
) gives us a means to compute the coefficients
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}
,
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),2}
, and
c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}
of the genus expansions (
4.3.32
) of the associated mixed cumulants
\tilde{c}_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{n}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1})}
.

With
\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}
drawn from the global scaled
(N,N)
Hermitised Laguerre ensemble defined in Propositions
3.16
and
3.18
, the relevant analogues of equations (
D.1.2
), (
D.1.3
), (
D.1.5
) are

\displaystyle c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\frac{1}{N}\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\right>,

(D.2.2)

\displaystyle c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),2}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\left(N\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\right>-N^{2}c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}\right),

(D.2.3)

\displaystyle c_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}

\displaystyle=\lim_{N\to\infty}\left(\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{2}}\right>-\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{1}}\right>\left<\Tr\,\tilde{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{k_{2}}\right>\right).

(D.2.4)

Here, we have used the fact (recall the discussion following the proof of Proposition
3.16
) that the
l=1
coefficient
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),1}
within the expansion (
4.3.32
) is zero. Like in the case of the antisymmetrised Laguerre ensemble, the cumulant expansion coefficients (
D.2.2
)–(
D.2.4
) have combinatorial interpretations as enumerations of certain ribbon graphs and topological hypermaps due to Propositions
3.16
and
3.18
(see also Figures
3.22
and
4.5
).

Recall from Lemma
3.5
that
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1})}
, consequently
\tilde{c}_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1})}
and, in turn,
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),0}
and
c_{k_{1}}^{(\mathcal{H}_{1}),2}
, vanish whenever
k_{1}
is odd. Hence, we display the values of these expansion coefficients obtained through equation (
